Otherwise returns total time above threshold.\n    \"\"\"\n    if df_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    g = df_tc.sort_values(\"time_s\")\n    t = g[\"time_s\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    y = g[\"temp_c\"].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n\n    # Check if crossing threshold\n        crosses = (y0 <= thr < y1) or (y1 <= thr < y0)\n\n        if y0 > thr and y1 > thr:\n            # Both above\n            if not in_segment:\n                in_segment = True\n                segment_start_time = t0\n                segment_start_temp = y0\n        elif crosses and y1 != y0:\n            # Crossing threshold\n            frac = (thr - y0) / (y1 - y0)\n            tcross = t0 + frac * (t1 - t0)\n\n            if y0 <= thr and y1 > thr:\n                # Entering segment above threshold\n                in_segment = True\n                segment_start_time = tcross\n            elif y0 > thr and y1 <= thr:\n                # Exiting segment above threshold\n                if in_segment:\n                    duration = tcross - segment_start_time\n                    segments.append(duration)\n                    in_segment = False\n        elif y0 <= thr and y1 <= thr:\n            # Both below\n            if in_segment:\n                duration = t0 - segment_start_time\n                segments.append(duration)\n                in_segment = False\n\n    # Handle case where we're still in a segment at the end\n    if in_segment:\n        duration = t[-1] - segment_start_time\n        segments.append(duration)\n\n    if not segments:\n        return None\n\n    if continuous_only:\n        # Return the longest continuous segment (main reflow)\n        return max(segments)\n    else:\n        # Return total time above threshold\n        return sum(segments)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n[Q1] Computing preheat ramp rates...\")\n\nq1_data = {\n    \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": cfg[\"ramp_limit_c_per_s\"],\n    \"violating_runs\": [],\n    \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n}\n\n# Compute for each run\nfor run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n    run_tc = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        continue\n\n    # Compute ramp for each TC and select the representative one (max ramp, tie-break by TC id)\n    ramps = []\n    for tc_id in sorted(run_tc[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_data = run_tc[run_tc[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n        ramp = max_slope_in_temp_band(tc_data, cfg[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmin\"], cfg[\"preheat_region\"][\"tmax\"])\n        if ramp is not None:\n            ramps.append((ramp, tc_id))\n\n    if ramps:\n        # Select max ramp with smallest tc_id as tie-breaker\n        # Sort by (ramp descending, tc_id ascending)\n        ramps_sorted = sorted(ramps, key=lambda x: (-x[0], x[1]))\n        max_ramp = ramps_sorted[0]\n        tc_id = max_ramp[1]\n        ramp_val = r2(max_ramp[0])\n\n        q1_data[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": ramp_val\n        }\n\n        if ramp_val is not None and ramp_val > cfg[\"ramp_limit_c_per_s\"]:\n            q1_data[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n# Sort violating runs\nq1_data[\"violating_runs\"] = sorted(q1_data[\"violating_runs\"])\n\n# Write Q1\nwith open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"q01.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(q1_data, f, indent=2)\nprint(f\"Q1: {len(q1_data['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_data['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n[Q2] Computing Time Above Liquidus...\")\n\nq2_data = []\n\n# Get solder liquidus from MES or use handbook default\n# Check if runs have solder_liquidus_c column\nif \"solder_liquidus_c\" in runs.columns:\n    for run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n        run_data = runs[runs[\"run_id\"] == run_id].iloc[0]\n        liquidus = run_data.get(\"solder_liquidus_c\", cfg[\"solder_liquidus_c\"])\n\n        run_tc = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n        if run_tc.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Compute TAL for each TC\n        # TAL measures total time above liquidus during the reflow profile\n        for tc_id in sorted(run_tc[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n            tc_data = run_tc[run_tc[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n            tal = time_above_threshold_s(tc_data, liquidus)\n\n            if tal is not None:\n                tal_val = r2(tal)\n                status = \"compliant\" if (cfg[\"tal_min_s\"] <= tal <= cfg[\"tal_max_s\"]) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n                q2_data.append({\n                    \"run_id\": run_id,\n                    \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n                    \"tal_s\": tal_val,\n                    \"required_min_tal_s\": r2(cfg[\"tal_min_s\"]),\n                    \"required_max_tal_s\": r2(cfg[\"tal_max_s\"]),\n                    \"status\": status\n                })\n\n# Sort by run_id, tc_id\nq2_data = sorted(q2_data, key=lambda x: (x[\"run_id\"], x[\"tc_id\"]))\n\nwith open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"q02.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(q2_data, f, indent=2)\nprint(f\"Q2: {len(q2_data)} TAL records computed\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n[Q3] Computing peak temperatures...\")\n\nq3_data = {\n    \"failing_runs\": [],\n    \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n}\n\nfor run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n    run_tc = tc[tc[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        q3_data[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n        continue\n\n    # Get peak for each TC\n    peaks = []\n    for tc_id in sorted(run_tc[\"tc_id\"].unique()):\n        tc_data = run_tc[run_tc[\"tc_id\"] == tc_id]\n        peak = tc_data[\"temp_c\"].max()\n        peaks.append((peak, tc_id))\n\n    if peaks:\n        # Select min peak (coldest) with smallest tc_id as tie-breaker\n        # Sort by (peak ascending, tc_id ascending)\n        peaks_sorted = sorted(peaks, key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))\n        min_peak = peaks_sorted[0]\n        tc_id = min_peak[1]\n        peak_val = r2(min_peak[0])\n\n        # Check if it meets requirement (peak margin above liquidus)\n        # Get liquidus from MES\n        liquidus = cfg[\"solder_liquidus_c\"]\n        if \"solder_liquidus_c\" in runs.columns:\n            run_data = runs[runs[\"run_id\"] == run_id].iloc[0]\n            liquidus = run_data.get(\"solder_liquidus_c\", liquidus)\n\n        required_min = r2(liquidus + cfg[\"peak_margin_c\"])\n\n        q3_data[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": peak_val,\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": required_min\n        }\n\n        if peak_val is not None and peak_val < required_min:\n            q3_data[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n    else:\n        q3_data[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n# Sort failing runs\nq3_data[\"failing_runs\"] = sorted(q3_data[\"failing_runs\"])\n\nwith open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"q03.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(q3_data, f, indent=2)\nprint(f\"Q3: {len(q3_data['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_data['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n[Q4] Computing conveyor speed feasibility...\")\n\nq4_data = []\n\n# The handbook provided limited conveyor guidance.\n# We'll use thermal mass index and board dimensions as proxies for dwell requirements\n# Strategy: Use board dimensions and typical dwell times based on thermal mass\n\nfor run_id in sorted(runs[\"run_id\"].unique()):\n    run_data = runs[runs[\"run_id\"] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n    # Get board dimensions and thermal mass index\n    board_length_cm = run_data.get(\"board_length_cm\", None)\n    board_width_cm = run_data.get(\"board_width_cm\", None)\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_data.get(\"thermal_mass_idx\", None)\n    actual_speed = run_data.get(\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\", None)\n\n    # Estimate effective heated length based on board dimensions and mass\n    # Typical oven heated length varies by model, but we can estimate based on recipe\n    # For now, use a conservative estimate based on thermal mass\n\n    if actual_speed is not None and thermal_mass_idx is not None:\n        # Dwell time requirement scales with thermal mass\n        # Heavier boards (higher thermal mass) need longer dwell\n        # Typical: minimum dwell 30-60s for heavy boards\n        min_dwell_s = 30 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.5) * 40  # Scale from 0.5 to 1.25\n        min_dwell_s = max(20, min(80, min_dwell_s))  # Clamp to reasonable range\n\n        # Estimate effective heated length from oven model\n        # Common heated lengths: ~90cm (8-zone), ~114cm (10-zone)\n        oven_model = run_data.get(\"oven_model\", \"\")\n        if \"8Z\" in oven_model:\n            heated_length_cm = 91.4  # 36 inches\n        elif \"10Z\" in oven_model:\n            heated_length_cm = 114.3  # 45 inches\n        else:\n            heated_length_cm = 100  # Default estimate\n\n        # Minimum speed: heated_length / min_dwell * 60\n        # Maximum speed: heated_length / max_dwell * 60\n        max_dwell_s = min_dwell_s + 30  # Allow some margin\n\n        required_min_speed = (heated_length_cm / max_dwell_s) * 60\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        q4_data.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": r2(required_min_speed),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": r2(actual_speed),\n            \"meets\": bool(meets)\n        })\n\n# Sort by run_id\nq4_data = sorted(q4_data, key=lambda x: x[\"run_id\"])\n\nwith open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"q04.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(q4_data, f, indent=2)\nprint(f\"Q4: {len(q4_data)} runs analyzed\")\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n[Q5] Selecting best run per board family...\")\n\nq5_data = []\n\n# Group by board_family\nboard_families = sorted(runs[\"board_family\"].unique())\n\nfor bf in board_families:\n    bf_runs = runs[runs[\"board_family\"] == bf][\"run_id\"].unique()\n\n    if len(bf_runs) == 0:\n        continue\n\n    # Score each run: quality (yield) and efficiency (speed)\n    scores = []\n    for run_id in bf_runs:\n        run_data = runs[runs[\"run_id\"] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get yield from defects\n        run_defects = defects[defects[\"run_id\"] == run_id]\n        yield_summary = run_defects[run_defects[\"inspection_stage\"] == \"SUMMARY\"]\n\n        if not yield_summary.empty:\n            yield_pct = yield_summary.iloc[0].get(\"fp_yield_pct\", 90.0)\n        else:\n            yield_pct = 90.0  # Default if not found\n\n        # Efficiency: conveyor speed (higher is better)\n        speed = run_data.get(\"conveyor_speed_cm_min\", 50)\n\n        # Composite score: 70% quality, 30% speed\n        # Normalize speed (assume range 0-100 cm/min)\n        normalized_speed = min(100, speed)\n        score = (yield_pct * 0.7) + (normalized_speed * 0.3)\n\n        # Check if run meets all compliance requirements\n        compliant = True\n\n        # Check Q1 (preheat ramp)\n        if run_id in q1_data[\"violating_runs\"]:\n            compliant = False\n\n        # Check Q3 (peak temp)\n        if run_id in q3_data[\"failing_runs\"]:\n            compliant = False\n\n        scores.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"yield_pct\": yield_pct,\n            \"speed\": speed,\n            \"score\": score,\n            \"compliant\": compliant\n        })\n\n    if scores:\n        # Filter to compliant runs only, then sort by score\n        compliant_scores = [s for s in scores if s[\"compliant\"]]\n\n        if compliant_scores:\n            scores = compliant_scores\n\n        scores = sorted(scores, key=lambda x: (-x[\"score\"], x[\"run_id\"]))\n\n        best_run = scores[0][\"run_id\"]\n        runner_ups = [s[\"run_id\"] for s in scores[1:]]\n\n        q5_data.append({\n            \"board_family\": bf,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_ups\n        })\n\n# Sort by board_family\nq5_data = sorted(q5_data, key=lambda x: x[\"board_family\"])\n\nwith open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"q05.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(q5_data, f, indent=2)\nprint(f\"Q5: {len(q5_data)} board families processed\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*70)\nprint(\"ANALYSIS COMPLETE\")\nprint(\"=\"*70)\nprint(f\"Output files written to {OUT_DIR}\")\nprint(\"  - q01.json: Preheat ramp rates\")\nprint(\"  - q02.json: Time Above Liquidus\")\nprint(\"  - q03.json: Peak temperatures\")\nprint(\"  - q04.json: Conveyor speed feasibility\")\nprint(\"  - q05.json: Best runs per board family\")\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":237,"oldLines":10,"newStart":237,"newLines":10,"lines":["             continue"," ","         #
